- **For the user: Q4 is still open.** *(Q1 and Q2 were open when this phase ended; both were answered on 2026-11-14, section 3. N5 and N8 were written to run against either answer of Q1.)*
- **Phase 3**: delete removes the annotation (`DELETED_COMMENT_RETURNS_ON_RERENDER`); the external manager must stop reading "no card in the DOM in the task that added it" as "line not rendered" (`THREAD_ADDED_WHILE_DIFF_AWAITS_DISPLAY`, H60: one page load is enough to hit it).
- **Phase 5**: `LAYOUT_TOGGLE_KEEPS`, `UPGRADE_WAITS_FOR_FOCUS`, `SMALL_CONTEXT_GAP` and `contextGapLabel` (new with D2 = B; `CONTEXT_FILE_PLACEMENT` no longer flips), `engineSettled` in the adapter (H59), the landings of H48 and H55.
- **Phase 6**: `BASE_CHANGE_KEEPS_CARDS` (probably), `GUTTER_BUTTON_STAYS_WITH_ANALYSIS_SUGGESTIONS` (H66), the gutter-button drag (H45), the suggestion block's extra line (H52).

### Phase 3: data-first refactors on the current engine

Goal: every consumer decides from data and resolves elements through one helper, proven by the phase-2 suite staying green on `FileDiff`. Application code only where the plan lists it; the vendor entry, the layout CSS, `renderFileDiff` and the lazy-body machinery are untouched, and `renderDiff` changed in two lines (its teardown and its empty message go through the new helpers).

What changed, by plan step. Each shared function is listed with its callers and what the change means for them.

1. **Data is written first.**
   - `PRManager.patchUserComment(id, updates)` (callers: `saveEditedUserComment`, `deleteUserComment`, and through them the card buttons, the review panel's quick actions and Clear All's reload) now also keeps the diff's own record in step (`_patchCommentAnnotation`): an edit is written to the comment object the annotation holds, a dismissal removes the annotation (`bridge.removeAnnotation(file, 'comment-<id>')`). For the edit caller nothing visible changes (the card's text is already updated in place); for the delete caller the file is re-rendered once without the comment, which is the fix of `DELETED_COMMENT_RETURNS_ON_RERENDER`. File-level comments and comments of files the bridge does not hold have no annotation and are left alone.
   - `PRManager._setSuggestionStatus(id, status)` writes `status` to the suggestion object the annotation holds. Callers: `_notifyAdoption` (adopt and edit-and-adopt, after the adopting comment is registered), `dismissSuggestion`, `restoreSuggestion`, `deleteUserComment` (the server dismissed the parent suggestion), `clearAllUserComments`. Each calls it after it has updated the card that exists; `_setSuggestionStatus` then records the card as showing that status (`markSuggestionSynced`), so the next draw applies nothing (H71). It looks the card up without building it and without syncing it.
2. **`sync` on every render of an annotation**, cache hit included: `PierreBridge._syncAnnotationElement`, called from `_renderAnnotation` (callers: the vendor's `renderAnnotation` callback, and `getAnnotationElement` only to build a card that does not exist yet; a lookup of a card that exists does not sync it, H71). Comments: `PostedComment.syncCommentElement` (status and publication stamps, hidden-as-posted, the edited-since-posted note, an update in flight). Suggestions: `SuggestionManager.syncSuggestionElement` (a status the card missed). Other types: an optional `sync` given to `registerAnnotationRenderer(type, fn, { sync })`; none is registered yet. Every sync is idempotent and leaves a card that already matches its data untouched, so the golden dump is unchanged.
3. **One way to get a card.** `PierreBridge.getAnnotationElement(file, id, { create })` returns the element the bridge holds for an annotation whether or not the diff has attached it, and with `create` builds it through the path the vendor callback uses (the vendor later gets that same element). A card that exists is handed back unchanged. With it: `hasAnnotation`, `findAnnotationFile`, `findAnnotationByData`, and `whenAnnotationRendered(file, id, { timeoutMs, isCancelled })`. On top of it:
   - `PRManager.findCommentCard(id, { fileLevel })` and `findSuggestionCard(id)`: bridge, then the zone registry, then the document (rows of the table renderer). They replace the by-id `document.querySelector` in `editUserComment`, `saveEditedUserComment`, `cancelEditUserComment`, `deleteUserComment`, `collapseSuggestionForAdoption`, `editAndAdoptSuggestion`, `adoptSuggestion`, `dismissSuggestion`, `restoreSuggestion`, `toggleOriginalSuggestion`, `_handleCommentFormSubmit` (the minimizer's expand of the new card, after the reload has settled) and `SuggestionUI.updateDismissedSuggestionUI`.
   - `ExternalCommentManager.findThreadCard(threadId, source)` and `threadAnnotationId`; its `_pierreContainer` / `_queryPierreRow` (`fileState.container` queries) are gone. `TourRenderer._queryPierreRow` asks the bridge; its `_pierreContainer` is gone. No module reads `pierreBridge.files.get(f).container` any more.
   - `PostedComment.findRenderedComments` includes the page's card for the comment and the cards in registered zones, attached or not; `syncEditedSincePosted` and `setUpdateBusy` reach them through it. Callers: `applyServerComment`, `updatePostedCommentOnHost`.
4. **The visibility oracle decides, not a DOM query after the add** (plan 3.9): `ExternalCommentManager._renderThreadPierre` (H60, flipped) and `TourRenderer._mountStopPierre` (H76).
5. **`PRManager.setFileCollapsed(path, bool)` and `isFileCollapsed(path)`** (H67). `toggleFileCollapse` (callers: header chevron and click, `toggleGeneratedFile`, the tour's expand on mount and re-collapse on exit) and `toggleFileViewed` (the Viewed checkbox) call them; `revealInDiff` expands through them. Both resolve the path they are given to the file's canonical path first (`_canonicalFilePath`, H78), so a caller may pass the path a finding or a thread carries. New for every caller: an expand that is waiting for the file's body is superseded by a later request for the same file (a per-path request counter), where main applied both in arrival order.
6. **One registry, one resolver for file-comment zones.** `FileCommentManager` registers every zone it creates (`getZone`, `forEachZone`, `releaseZone`, `releaseAllZones`; lifecycle in H75); `PRManager.getFileCommentsZone(path)` and `forEachFileCommentsZone(fn)` are the only lookups. Converted: `FileCommentManager.loadFileComments` (callers: `_loadUserComments`, `_removeRenderedSuggestions`, the suggestion display) and `findZoneForFile`, `CommentMinimizer._refreshFileIndicators`, `ExternalCommentManager._resolveFileCommentsZone` and its `clear()` sweep, `applyPostedCommentVisibility` (the class sweep and the recount), the two `findZoneForFile` calls of the adopt flows. A unit test fails on any `querySelector` for `.file-comments-zone` in `public/js`.
7. **`PRManager.revealInDiff(target)`**, the one navigation helper: expand the file if collapsed, render its body, reveal the line range, resolve the card (annotation id, waiting until it is attached; else the file's comment zone; else the caller's last resort), expand it in minimize mode, scroll with `scrollIntoViewStable`. Cancelled after every await through the caller's generation token. Callers: `AIPanel.scrollToFinding`, `scrollToComment` (and through it `ReviewHistoryModal.jumpToComment`), `scrollToExternalThread`, `SuggestionNavigator.goToSuggestion`, `TourRenderer.scrollToStop`. `AIPanel.expandFileIfCollapsed` and `_scrollDiffTarget`, and `SuggestionNavigator.ensureSuggestionVisible` and `scrollToSuggestion`, were their copies of parts of the sequence and are deleted. For the callers: the finding, comment and thread are found by identity (annotation id, thread id) before any document query; a card that the diff has not attached yet (a pending diff) is waited for instead of being reported missing.
8. **`PRManager._setDiffStatus(html | null, { hideScroller })`**, `_clearDiffStatus(token)` and `_removeRenderedFiles()`; `#diff-status` in `pr.html` and `local.html`, before an empty `#diff-container`. The eight `innerHTML` writers of the plan (`pr.js` five, `local.js` three) are gone; a unit test fails on any markup write to the container and was checked against the phase-2 tree, where it finds exactly those eight. Callers of `_setDiffStatus`: `renderDiff` (clears; "No files changed"), `loadAndDisplayFiles` (two error messages), `refreshPR` (loading, and its own message taken down in `finally`, H74), Local `loadLocalDiff` (empty scope, error).
9. **`findFileElement` audit** (H73) and `PRManager.isFileInDiffPane(path)`.
10. **Local whitespace, refresh and scope change call `_rerenderAllOverlays`** (H68). The base-branch change is as on main (`BASE_CHANGE_KEEPS_CARDS` is still `false`).

Tests. New unit files: `find-card`, `set-file-collapsed`, `reveal-in-diff`, `diff-status` (with the two grep tests and a check of both pages' markup), `file-comments-zone-registry` (with its grep test), `posted-comment-sync`, `suggestion-sync`. Extended: `pierre-bridge-annotation-reuse` (sync on a cache hit, `getAnnotationElement`, the lookups, `whenAnnotationRendered` with a stepped frame clock), `file-comment-manager` (the registry), `pr-external-comments-wiring` (Local mode), `comment-minimizer` (a detached zone). Rewritten around the real bridge and the real navigation helper (H72): the Pierre sections of `external-comment-manager` and `tour-renderer`, `SuggestionNavigator`, `ai-panel-scroll-race`, `ai-panel-scroll-render`, `ai-panel-external-segment`, the AI panel part of `inline-feedback-context`. Adapted: `file-level-ai-cards-reload`, `submitted-comment-ui`, `suggestion-status`, `refresh-comments-reload`. Each new helper has a happy path, missing input and an error or refusal case. `tests/CONVENTIONS.md` has the two new rules (the vendor-free bridge harness; the page manager).

E2E: two scenarios were added in round 2, each in both modes (below); no spec file was added. Changed: the two constants in `tests/e2e/diff-dom.js`; a `diffStatus(page)` helper there, used by the one assertion that read the empty-diff message out of `#diff-container` (`diff-render.spec.js`).

Deviations from the plan in this phase, with the proof:

- **`ChatPanel._scrollToLine` is not on `revealInDiff`; six of the plan's seven callers are.** `revealInDiff` renders the file's body before anything else. Routed through it, a chat link to a file that has never rendered arrives, which is `CHAT_LINK_REACHES_UNRENDERED_FILE`, and the plan's phase 7 form of the helper (wait until the line is displayed, then flash) is what `REVEALED_LINE_KEEPS_FLASH` asserts. This ledger assigns both flips to phase 7 (H51), and the instruction for this phase was to flip only what the ledger gives to phase 3 and leave every other characterization as it is. Keeping the two defects alive inside the shared helper with a switch for one caller would have been worse than leaving that caller alone. `ChatPanel._scrollToLine`, `_handleFileLinkClick` and `ChatPanel.js` are untouched. **Phase 7 migrates this caller and flips both constants.**
- **`revealInDiff` takes more than the plan's six fields**: `findElement` (a last resort for rows of the table renderer, which neither the bridge nor a zone holds; it goes with that renderer in phase 8), `prepare`, `isCancelled`, `scroll`, `expandMinimized`. It returns `{ element, cancelled }`; the flash stays with the caller because the three callers flash different things.
- **`whenAnnotationRendered` waits for "attached", not for "attached with a box"** (plan 3.10). A card that minimize mode hides has no box and is still the card to hand back (the line is scrolled to instead), so waiting for a box would cost four seconds on every navigation in minimize mode. And it does not wait at all for a line the diff does not show (H69).
- **`isFileCollapsed` asks the wrapper on this engine** (H67). The plan's `setFileCollapsed` "needs no wrapper element"; on this engine the collapsed state of generated and viewed files exists nowhere else. The single-writer, single-reader shape is in place and the callers will not change again.
- **The suggestion sync applies transitions only** (H71), where the plan's wording is "re-stamps ... suggestion status" on every call.
- **`renderDiff` was touched in two places** although the plan lists it under "not touched": its `innerHTML = ''` and its "No files changed" are two of the eight writers step 8 removes. Nothing else in it changed.
- **Three lines of phase-2 specs changed.** `diff-render.spec.js` reads the empty-diff message through `diffStatus(page)`: plan 7.3 says specs change once, in phase 2, and this one named `#diff-container` as the place of a message that step 8 moves. `chat-file-links.spec.js` and `scroll-landing.spec.js` call `test.slow()` in their read-through tests (H77): a time budget, no assertion touched.
- **The unit tests of the bridge's consumers were rebuilt on the production bridge, not on a new stub** (H72), which is more than the plan's "rewrite around the visibility oracle and `getAnnotationElement`".
- **User-visible changes beyond the two flips**, all in rare states and none covered by a characterization: a tour stop in a file whose diff awaits display is shown instead of skipped (H76); navigation from the review panel waits for a card that is not attached yet instead of doing nothing; a failed or empty pull-request refresh that does not re-render leaves the old diff instead of "Refreshing pull request..." (H74); while a refresh runs the old diff is hidden, not destroyed; an "Update on GitHub" button drawn while the update runs is disabled (H70); the empty-diff message is a box above the container instead of its first child (same look: one box; with context files present the message is on top of them as before).

Round 2: the two findings of the verifier's rejection, and what was done (evidence directory `phase-3/round2`; the verifier's own material is in `phase-3/verify`).

1. **A collapsed file reached through a path in another form was left half-expanded, in both modes.** Reproduced by the verifier with `aiPanel.scrollToFinding(id, './src/utils.js', line)` on a collapsed `src/utils.js`: the wrapper lost its `collapsed` class while `collapsedFiles` and the bridge's record kept the file collapsed, so the header showed it open and the body had no line. Main ended with all three expanded. Cause and fix: H78. `setFileCollapsed` and `isFileCollapsed` keyed their state by the string they were given and found the wrapper through the tolerant `findFileElement`; the two navigation callers of main canonicalized by hand and were replaced in step 7 by `revealInDiff`, which passes the caller's path. Both functions now resolve their argument with `PRManager._canonicalFilePath` before anything else. Callers and what changes for each: `toggleFileCollapse` (header, `toggleGeneratedFile`, the tour's expand and re-collapse) and `toggleFileViewed` pass canonical paths and take the first branch of the resolver, so nothing changes for them; `revealInDiff` (review panel, navigator, tour) gets main's end state back for a path in another form. Concurrency: the per-path request counter is keyed by the canonical path, so an expand requested as `./a.js` and a collapse requested as `a.js` supersede each other (unit test).
2. **A card lookup applied suggestion status transitions, and H71 described the opposite.** `getAnnotationElement` went through `_renderAnnotation`, which syncs on a cache hit, so `findSuggestionCard` and `_setSuggestionStatus` ran `syncSuggestionElement` inside the adopt, dismiss, restore and delete flows. Visible consequence found by the verifier: an adopted suggestion the reader had chosen to show again was collapsed when its adopting comment was deleted (main leaves it open, label "Hide"); and `dismissSuggestion` left `data-hidden-for-adoption="false"` where main leaves the attribute absent. Decision: main's behaviour is kept, because this phase may change what the reader sees only where the ledger says so. Fix: `getAnnotationElement` hands back a card that exists unchanged and calls `_renderAnnotation` only to build one that does not; the sync runs when a card is built and when the vendor draws it. H71 and H18 are rewritten to say what the code does. Callers of `getAnnotationElement` and what changes for each: `findCommentCard`, `findSuggestionCard`, `ExternalCommentManager.findThreadCard`, `TourRenderer._queryPierreRow` and `whenAnnotationRendered` (all with `create`) get the same element as before, without a sync when it already existed; `_setSuggestionStatus` (without `create`) likewise. None of them relied on the sync: the comment flows write their changes to the card themselves, and no external-thread or tour-stop renderer registers a `sync`.

Tests added in round 2:

- Unit: `set-file-collapsed.test.js`, a describe over the real `DiffRenderer.findFileElement` (`./`, `/` and git rename forms for the reader and the writer; the variant is never written to `collapsedFiles`; requests under two forms supersede each other; a file without a wrapper is resolved from the changed-file list; a variant of no file is refused; `_canonicalFilePath` itself, with missing and non-string input). `reveal-in-diff.test.js`: one case over the real collapse functions with `./src/a.js`. `pierre-bridge-annotation-reuse.test.js`: "a lookup never syncs a card that exists; only building it and the engine's draw do". `find-card.test.js`: a describe over the real `restoreSuggestion`, `deleteUserComment`, `dismissSuggestion` and the real suggestion manager (shown adopted, then delete; dismiss and restore leave no adoption marker; neither lookup calls the sync; a card no flow handled is still corrected by a draw). Run against the rejected tree of the first report, twelve of the new cases fail (eight of the variant-path cases, the four lookup cases) and the other 74 tests of those four files pass (`round2/new-unit-tests-on-round1-tree.log`).
- E2E, each in both modes: `scroll-landing.spec.js` "an AI-panel click on a finding that names its file in another form expands the collapsed file completely" (a seeded finding with file `./<path>`, clicked in the review panel; then the reader expands a gap, which re-renders the file, and uses the header twice) and `offscreen-mutations.spec.js` "an adopted suggestion shown again stays open when its adopting comment is deleted, also after its file is re-rendered" (through the card's own buttons). The first assertion one would write for the first scenario, "the file shows its rows after the click", does not detect the defect: on this engine a collapsed file keeps its rows in the DOM until its next re-render, so they reappear with the wrapper class whatever the engine was told. The gap expansion is what makes an engine still told "collapsed" draw nothing.
- Both scenarios were run on three trees (`round2/new-scenarios-on-*.log`): the phase-2 tree (`git archive HEAD`, with its own `diff-dom.js`, where `DELETED_COMMENT_RETURNS_ON_RERENDER` is `true`): 4 of 4 pass, so they state main's behaviour; the rejected tree of the first report: 4 of 4 fail, at "Expected: > 45, Received: 0" rows after the gap expansion and at the suggestion card having the class `collapsed`; this tree: 4 of 4 pass.
- The verifier's probes on this tree (`round2/verifier-probe-*.log`): the variant-path probe ends with wrapper, bridge record and `collapsedFiles` all expanded and 25 rows, in both modes, as on the phase-2 tree; the shown-adopted probe ends open with "Hide", in both modes, as on the phase-2 tree; the full adopt, show, re-render, delete, restore, dismiss sequence differs from the phase-2 tree in four lines, all of them the number of comment cards after the re-render (1 here, 2 there), which is the `DELETED_COMMENT_RETURNS_ON_RERENDER` flip.

### Phase 4: the new bridge, in isolation

Goal: a bridge whose behaviour is proven against the real vendor before the app uses it. No page loads the new bridge; what the app runs changed in three places, each proven to change nothing a reader sees (the F23 correction, the card renderers and CSS moving into shared modules, `createSuggestionRow` taking its status from one rule).

**First, the D2 amendment was transferred into this ledger** (sections 3, 4, 6 items 10 and 11, 7, 8, 9, 10 and the new section 12). Its hazards are H79 to H92 here; the mapping from the plan's numbers is above H67.

**Then the F23 correction, alone, with its proof** (ledger H87, plan H70). `PierreContext.mergeContextRanges` writes `splitLineStart` and `unifiedLineStart` as the running total plus the hunk's own `collapsedBefore`. On the tree with only that change (and the two test-infrastructure fixes below): full E2E 852 passed / 2 skipped / 0 failed (`e2e-f23-only.log`, `.meta.txt`), golden dump compared with phase 3's: "compared 32 dump(s)", "no regression" (`golden-f23-only/`, `golden-f23-only-compare.txt`). Unit: `pierre-context.test.js` holds every hunk of the output to the vendor's formula on a hydrated diff and on a zero-hunk base, for one range, two ranges, a range touching a hunk and ranges around the hunks (82 tests in the file). Contract: `vendor/context-items.spec.js` reads the formula from the vendor's own output (`parsePatchFiles`, `hydratePartialDiff`) and shows the layout exact after manual expansions with the corrected starts and a row off with the old ones (H95). The same module gained `rangesFromExpansionMap(metadata, map, { side, threshold })`, the pure fold of the vendor's expansion map into ranges (clamped to the gap, `Infinity` for a whole gap, gaps of five lines or fewer always).

What was built:

- **`public/js/modules/pierre-codeview-bridge.js`** (`window.PierreCodeViewBridge`). One `CodeView`, records of kind `diff` and `context` in `files` (keyed by path, in pane order), and:
  - the two list writers `renderAll(entries, { keyPrefix })` and `reconcile(entries, { whenIdle })` over `_requestListWrite` and `_writeList()`, the only caller of `setItems`, which sends the records of `files` as they are when it runs; asked for from inside a viewer callback it runs in a microtask after the pass, under the five rules of H103 (clear, set, flush, restore: first line below the sticky header by a line target, or the item when no row is in view or the line is no longer shown; line selection; focused element and caret; hover). `reconcile` keeps every record whose id and kind are in the entries, folds manual expansions into ranges before a list write, writes no list when the ids are unchanged, refuses entries whose changed files are not the pane's (H79), and with `whenIdle` waits while a pointer button is down on the pane or `holdListWrites()` is in place;
  - publish and flush: one `updateItem` and one `render(true)` per change, one flush per `batch(fn)`, a microtask flush when the viewer is inside one of its callbacks (H99);
  - annotations: `addAnnotation(s)`, `removeAnnotation`, `removeAnnotationsByType`, `getAnnotations`, `hasAnnotation`, `findAnnotationFile`, `findAnnotationByData`, `getAnnotationElement(file, id, { create })`, `whenAnnotationRendered`, `registerAnnotationRenderer(type, fn, { sync })`; the file-comment zone (`file-comments`) is the first annotation of every item and a `notice` the second (`setNotice`, a deferred entry's `notice`); the zone's side is the file's status first, the parsed type second;
  - contents and ranges: `upgradeFileContents` by diff type (`upgradeStrategy`: hydrate, none, reparse; H97), `materializeDeferred`, `addContextRanges`, `convertOldToNew`, `isLineVisible` (from the published metadata and the instance's expansion map), `isDiffDisplayed`, `whenLineDisplayed`; every derived metadata is re-keyed by `rekey(record, metadata)`;
  - context records: base `{ ...parseDiffFromFile(file, file), type: 'new' }`, stored ranges and revealed ranges merged by `mergeContextRanges`, every annotation published on the additions side with its stored side kept in its data, `lang: 'text'` above 400 KB or 12,000 lines or with a NUL character (`contextLanguage`), no diff position;
  - `setCollapsed`, `setDiffStyle`, `setTheme` (one options object, replaced; callbacks keep their identity), `scrollToItem`, `scrollToLine` (false when the data does not show the line; the flash survives row replacement, H96), `selectLines` / `getLineSelection` / `clearLineSelection`, `getHoveredLine` / `getHoveredRow`, `isPointerOverFile`, `hasFocusInside`, `findFileElement`, `refreshHostState`, `getDiffPosition`, `getCodeFromLines`, `destroy`;
  - host stamps (`data-file-name`, `d2h-file-wrapper`, `context-file`, `collapsed`, the `hostState` hook's classes) put on at mount and update, removed at unmount, remembered per host so that exactly what was stamped is removed;
  - hooks `renderHeader(id, kind)`, `hostState(id, kind)`, `onItemLifecycle(id, phase)`, `resolvePath(path)`; `collaborators` for the card renderers; the interaction callbacks of the current bridge. Nothing reads `window.prManager` (a unit test greps).
  - Every function that takes a file resolves the path once at its entry (`_record`: exact id, then the `resolvePath` hook, then a leading `./` or `/`), the rule of H78.
- **`pierre-annotations.js`**: the comment, suggestion, suggestion-fallback and form builders (moved from the current bridge, text unchanged, their collaborators now an argument), `sortAnnotations` and `TYPE_ORDER`, `applySplitAnnotationLayout`, and `AnnotationElements` (registry and element cache: `draw` builds and syncs, `lookup` never changes a card that exists; H71).
- **`pierre-styles.js`**: `ANNOTATION_CSS` (moved, text unchanged), `ROWLESS_FIX` (from the contract harness, which now imports it from here), `CONTEXT_CSS` (the ten verified rules), `unsafeCSS()`.
- **`pierre-gutter.js`**: the two gutter buttons with the button-drag selection, and the pure `resolveClickTarget(hovered, selection)`.

Shared functions that changed, and their callers:

- `PierreContext.mergeContextRanges` (callers: the current bridge's `_applyContextRanges`, from `addContextRanges` and `upgradeFileContents`, and `_rebuildFileInstance`; the new bridge's `_derive`): line starts in the vendor's convention. For the current bridge nothing on screen changes (proof above).
- `PierreBridge._renderCommentAnnotation`, `_renderSuggestionAnnotation`, `_renderSuggestionFallback`, `_renderFormAnnotation` (caller: `_renderAnnotationContent`, from the vendor callback and `getAnnotationElement`; gate G9), `_applySplitAnnotationLayout` (caller: `_syncSplitAnnotationLayout`), the sort in `_updateAnnotations`, and the statics `ANNOTATION_CSS` (callers: `getUnsafeCSS`, gate G9, a unit test), `SUGGESTION_ICON_SVG`, `CHAT_FORM_SVG`: each delegates to the shared module with what it read from the page before (`PierreBridge._pageEnv()`, resolved at each use, and `this.options` as the form's handlers). Same elements, same order, same CSS text.
- `SuggestionManager.createSuggestionRow` (callers: `displayAISuggestions`' row branch, `PRManager.createSuggestionRow`, the shared suggestion builder for both bridges): the card's status comes from `effectiveSuggestionStatus`, the rule `syncSuggestionElement` uses, where it computed the same thing a second time. Same card for every input; its debug `console.log` for an adopted suggestion is now printed only when a comment, and not the suggestion's own status, says "adopted".
- `tests/e2e/helpers.js` `mockAppConfig` (callers: context-files, offscreen-mutations, rerender-overlays specs) and `pushHunkSummaries` (offscreen-mutations, rerender-overlays): below.

Tests:

- **Bridge-in-browser** (`tests/contract/bridge`, 84 tests after the second fix round; `bridge-page.js` is `window.BH`, `bridge-test.js` the fixture, which also loads the app's own CSS for the gutter buttons cut out of `pr.css`): `render-all` (7: entry order, re-render in four modes with the reader put back at 0 px, host stamps through pooling, paths in another form), `annotations` (9: same-task attach, order on a line, cache across remount, sync on a draw and never on a lookup, a never-mounted card, the flush guard, `batch`, the app's real cards with collaborators handed in, the comment-restore scenario), `contents` (8: upgrade by type, a line of a pure rename revealed, the #582 cases with a real click on an expand control, a never-mounted reveal, a diff awaiting display, `whenLineDisplayed`), `context-records` (11), `reconcile` (11: a context file added above, directly above and below the reader and removed again at 0 px; no list write with unchanged ids, spied on the real instance; kept and destroyed records; focus, caret, selection and hover put back; the fold; a pending diff; the pool not ready; stale entries; `whenIdle`), `view` (8: the zone, status-decided side, split full width, collapse, layout and theme with a focused textarea, scroll targets, notice items, a worker that does not load, no pool and no bundle), `gutter` (7: hover, both buttons, button drag, selection API, split side, hide on scroll, hover back after an update), `list-write-in-callback` (23; H103. Eleven from the first fix round: a reconcile from the lifecycle hook and from a card renderer; what changes before the write is part of it; a direct write in between, as a new diff and as another reconcile; `destroy()` in between; two requests in one pass; a request made by a hook while a list write is drawing; a new diff from a hook, with a manual expansion on the old item; the old list drawn again before the write; scroll targets and a selection in between, against a direct reconcile. Twelve from the second: an item drawn for the first time between the request and the write, its file taken out by `reconcile`, dropped by a new diff, or replaced by a new diff of the same files; the view restore of a write whose flush made a hook ask for the next one, in four scenarios, each against two direct writes; the same with a kept context file whose ranges the nested reconcile changed; a scroll target and a selection sent by a hook while a write is drawing; an old item the viewer unmounts before the write, of a replaced record and of a file taken out; a list write asked for from the selection callback of a write's view restore).
- **Vendor contract** (`tests/contract/vendor/context-items.spec.js`, 17 tests): F20 (2), F21 (2, one of them "why `CONTEXT_CSS` exists"), F22 (4: the hidden "Expand all", every control by real clicks in unified and split, the label click), F23 (7), F24, F25. The label click, which the plan had only read in the source, reveals `expansionLineCount` lines at both ends of the gap.
- **Gate G13** (4 tests; section 5).
- **Unit**: `pierre-codeview-bridge.test.js` (81 after the second fix round, on the recording stub of `tests/utils/codeview-bridge-harness.js`: records and the first list, the options object, the flush state machine, a list write asked for inside a viewer callback (19: H103's rules as the bridge's own decisions), derivation by type and re-keying, the visibility oracle, context records, which records `reconcile` keeps and when it writes the list, paths in another form, scroll targets and selection, diff positions, and three greps: `setItems` only in `_writeList`, no splicing method of the viewer, no `window.prManager` and no position scroll target), `pierre-shared-modules.test.js` (32: display order, the builders with and without collaborators, `AnnotationElements`, the split pass, `resolveClickTarget` and the gutter buttons, the CSS blocks, the script order of both pages), `pierre-context.test.js` (82 tests; 55 before). `tests/CONVENTIONS.md` has four new rules (the bridge-in-browser suite, testing from inside a viewer callback, what a publish guarantees, the recording stub).

Two test-infrastructure flakes fixed at their cause (tests only), both seen once each in the verifier's phase-3 runs:

- **`hunk-summaries.spec.js` "shows generating pulse when job is in flight, clears on finish"** (`:245` then, `:247` now). The page registers its listeners for the server's pushes at the end of its load sequence (`PRManager._initReviewEventListeners`, after the suggestions and the running-analysis check), later than `waitForDiffToRender` returns; the test dispatched `review:background_job_finished` into a page that was not listening yet, and the pulse never cleared. `whenReviewEventsBound(page)` in `tests/e2e/helpers.js` waits for `prManager._reviewEventsBound`; the test, the spec's two dispatch helpers, `pushHunkSummaries` and `context-files.spec.js`'s `pushContextFilesChanged` wait for it before they dispatch.
- **`mockAppConfig` let a rejected `route.fetch()` leave the route handler** (`read ECONNRESET` on a kept-alive connection the test server had closed), an unhandled rejection that failed whichever test was running and restarted the worker (`rerender-overlays.spec.js:489` "failed at 0 ms" in the verifier's run). The handler now tries once more on a fresh connection, then fails the request for the page as it would have failed without the mock, and never throws.

Results of the first report (evidence directory `phase-4`; superseded by the fix round below, kept because the F23-only runs and the gate measurements are referred to above). One script, `final-sequence.sh`, ran the unit suite, two full E2E runs, the golden dump with its comparison and the contract suite one after the other on one tree (fingerprint over `tests` and `public` `e93d7a45…` at the start, before and after each E2E run, and at the end; `tree-fingerprint.txt`, `e2e-final-run{1,2}.meta.txt`):

- `pnpm run test:e2e`, two consecutive runs: 852 passed / 2 skipped / 0 failed, twice (854 tests, as after phase 3: no E2E test was added or removed). One-minute load average 8 to 9 at the end of each.
- Golden dump compared with phase 3's reference: 32 dumps, 0 unsettled files, 0 console errors, "compared 32 dump(s)", "no regression", exit 0 (`golden-final/`, `golden-final-dump.log`, `golden-final-compare.txt`).
- After those runs one more change was made, to a file no page loads and to its unit test, **and it is the change the first verification rejected** (H103; it had one test, on the recording stub, and none in a browser): a list write asked for from inside a viewer callback waits for the pass to end (`_writeList`; `pierre-codeview-bridge.test.js` "a list write asked for while the viewer is inside a render callback ..."). The fingerprint of everything else under `tests` and `public` is the same before and after it (`after-run-change.txt`), so the E2E runs and the dump stand; a third full E2E run on the final tree (fingerprint `74ffeed1…`, `e2e-final-tree.meta.txt`) gave 852 passed / 2 skipped / 0 failed as well. The unit and contract suites were run again on the final tree:
- `pnpm test`: 346 files, 11610 tests, all passing (`unit-final.log`; 344 files and 11488 tests after phase 3).
- `pnpm run test:contract`: 189 passed (`contract-final.log`; 107 after phase 3; +61 bridge, +17 vendor, +4 gate), with the height validator on in every bridge and gate test. Gate measurements re-recorded in `gates/`.

- Measured along the way, recorded and not asserted: a list replace with an item inserted above the reader, 31 items, pool on: 10.6 ms (`gates/F24-list-replace.json`; 12.8 ms in the planning probe); `parseDiffFromFile(file, file)` for 20,000 lines: 18.8 ms (`gates/F25-parse-cost.json`).

**Fix round after the first verification** (evidence directory `phase-4/round2`). The verifier rejected the phase on one finding: the list write deferred from inside a viewer callback published the list as it was when it was asked for (four silent failures on the real bundle; H103 has them). What was done:

- The deferral was rebuilt on five rules (H103; the bridge's file header states them): `_requestListWrite` and a `_writeList()` that builds the list from `files` when it runs; one waiting write at most, which a direct write or `destroy()` supersedes; `_listed`, the records the viewer's list was written from, as what every viewer callback resolves; no publish for a record that is not listed yet; scroll targets and selections wait for the write. `destroy()` is final.
- Proof in the browser: `bridge/list-write-in-callback.spec.js`, 11 tests on the real development bundle with the validator on. The verifier's own probe spec, unchanged, on the fixed bridge: all four scenarios and both controls consistent and sound, no error (`verifier-probe-after-fix.log`). The new spec repeated eight times: 88 passed (`list-write-spec-repeat.log`).
- Proof that the tests can fail: eleven mutations of the bridge, one per rule or part of a rule, M8 being the rejected implementation; every one fails the browser spec and the unit tests (`mutations/browser-mutations.log`, `mutations/unit-mutations.log`). The first mutation run was thrown away: two "kills" came from a wrong expectation in a test that had been edited after its last green run; the spec was re-run unmutated before the runs recorded here.
- A second design fault was found on the way and is why rule 3 reads as it does: making the callbacks answer nothing for an item whose record had been replaced satisfied the verifier's four scenarios and failed a fifth that this round added (the old list drawn again before the write, by a layout change in the same task), with validator errors (`stale-item-answering-nothing-validator.log`).

One script, `round2/sequence.sh`, ran everything one after the other on one tree (fingerprint over `tests` and `public` `60adc6fc…` at the start, before and after each E2E run and at the end; `tree-fingerprint.txt`, `e2e-run{1,2}.meta.txt`):

- `pnpm test`: 346 files, 11620 tests, all passing (`unit.log`; 11610 in the first report: the one stub test of the rejected deferral became eleven).
- `pnpm run test:contract`: 200 passed (`contract.log`; 189 in the first report, +11), validator on in every bridge and gate test; gate measurements re-recorded in `round2/gates/`.
- `pnpm run test:e2e`, two consecutive runs: 852 passed / 2 skipped / 0 failed, twice.
- Golden dump against phase 3's reference: 32 dumps, 0 unsettled files, 0 console errors, "compared 32 dump(s)", "no regression", exit 0 (`golden/`, `golden-dump.log`, `golden-compare.txt`).
- The same script had run once before, on the tree without the console-guard entry described next (`sequence-before-guard-entry/`, fingerprint `04fb2e59…`): unit and contract as above, golden "no regression", E2E 851 passed / 2 skipped / **1 failed** in the first run and 852 / 2 / 0 in the second. The failure was `team-reviews-filter.spec.js:103`, a test of the home page.

A third test-infrastructure flake, found in that run and fixed at its cause (tests only):

- **`team-reviews-filter.spec.js:103` "valid team persists across reload and restores the input value".** Applying a team filter starts two requests one after the other, the cached GET and then the live refresh (POST); the test reloads the page as soon as the filter is in localStorage. A reload that lands while the GET is in flight makes the page log `Error loading team-reviews: TypeError: Failed to fetch`, which the console guard has named since phase 2 as an interrupted loader; one that lands while the refresh is in flight logs `Error refreshing team-reviews: TypeError: Failed to fetch` from the same function chain, which the guard did not name, so the test failed. Which one it is depends on timing: it failed once in the eight full runs of this phase made before the entry was added (four in the first report, the verifier's two, the two of the first sequence here). Reproduced every time by a probe that holds the refresh POST and reloads (`team-reviews-probe/before-guard-entry.log`: both lines logged, the guard fails on the second). The guard's list of loaders a navigation can interrupt now names `Error refreshing team-reviews` as well; the entry is anchored on that prefix and on `TypeError: Failed to fetch`, so a refresh that fails for another reason, or the refresh of another collection, still fails the test (`e2e-console-guard.test.js`). After it: the probe passes with the same two lines logged (`after-guard-entry.log`), the spec repeated ten times solo passes (`spec-solo-repeat10.log`, 60 passed: its six tests ten times), and the two full runs above are on that tree. Nothing in this phase touches the home page; the flake has been possible since the guard was introduced.

**Second fix round, after the second verification** (evidence directory `phase-4/round3`). The verifier rejected the phase on two findings, both inside the window H103 is about (a list write is waiting, or drawing, and `files` is ahead of the viewer's list):

1. *An item drawn for the first time after its record had gone asked the page for its header.* Rule 3 kept "the header slot it has"; a record that was removed or replaced before its item was ever drawn has none, and the code fell through to `hooks.renderHeader` for a file the page had forgotten. The item was mounted without a header and the validator logged two errors (H103 rule 3 has the lines). The ledger's sentence and the returned deviation had stated the rule without that exception.
2. *The view restore of a list write decided from `files`.* When a hook asked for another list write during the first one's flush, `_restoreView` judged the next records while the viewer held the list just written: a line target for a line the item did not show (the reader ended 40 px off), a line target and a selection for an id the viewer had no item for.

What was done (all in `pierre-codeview-bridge.js`, still loaded by no page; H103 has the rules as they now read):

- Finding 1, at its root: a viewer callback about an item whose record is no longer the pane's never reaches the page, whether or not the item was drawn before. `_renderHeader` hands back the slot the record has, or an empty slot that sets its own height to `itemMetrics.diffHeaderHeight` (`_emptyHeaderSlot`). The same question was then asked of every other callback, and three more answers changed: `_renderAnnotation` hands back the card the annotation has and runs no renderer and no sync of the page (it ran both); `onLineSelected` and `onLineSelectionEnd` are forwarded only for a record that is the pane's (as `onLineClick` already was); and `'unmount'` is told by id, exactly when `'mount'` was told and the pane still has the file (`_mountsTold`; before, the old item of a replaced record that was first drawn in the window had its `'unmount'` told at the write without a `'mount'`, and one that the viewer unmounted before the write had its `'mount'` told and its `'unmount'` not).
- Finding 2, at its root: whatever the bridge sends to the viewer's list is decided from that list. `_restoreView`, `_scrollTo` and `selectLines` resolve the file in `_listed`; whether a line target can be sent is asked of the item the viewer holds (`_listShowsLine`: `codeView.getItem(id)`), not of a record, because a kept record is changed in place by `reconcile` and is ahead of its item until the waiting write. `isLineVisible` is unchanged (it answers from the pane's data) and shares `_showsLine` with it.
- Two more cases of the same window, found by asking what else a hook can do while a write is drawing: a scroll target or a selection sent there waited for nothing and was undone by the write's own view restore; it now runs after the restore (`_listWriteRunning`, `_onList`). And a list write asked for by the page from the selection callback of the view restore ran inside the write in progress; it now waits for it to end (`_requestListWrite`).
- The gutter show and hide loops go over `_listed` (the gutters are those of the items the viewer has).
- One existing unit expectation changed, deliberately: "a file taken out goes on drawing its item until the write" expected a card that had never been drawn to be built by the page's renderer for the removed file. It now expects the card that was drawn to be handed back as it is, and the one that never was to stay unbuilt, with the renderer and the sync not called.

Proof:

- Browser, on the real development bundle with the validator on: twelve tests added to `bridge/list-write-in-callback.spec.js` (inventory above). `BH.spyOnListCommands()` (new in `bridge-page.js`) records every `scrollTo` and `setSelectedLines` that reaches the real instance with what the viewer's own item held at that moment; `BH.rendererCalls` records every call of a registered renderer. The nested scenarios are each compared with a control run (two direct writes; the commands after the write): same first visible file, same pixel, same selection.
- The verifier's own probe specs, unchanged, on the final bridge (`round3/verifier-probes-after-fix.log`, 13 probes, no console error in any): no page hook asked about the forgotten file (V7's callback log is `header:file25.ts, mount:file25.ts, --reconcile asked--, header:file26.py, mount:file26.py`), the stale item 1132 px high as laid out; the nested restore sends the same targets as the control and ends at 0 px (V2); no target and no selection for an id the viewer has no item for (V4, V5). One thing to know when reading that log: in V6's "same files" variant the list named `asksAboutVictimBeforeWrite` is not empty. The probe's spies stay armed until its settle, and the entries are the new record's item being drawn after the write (its lifecycle list starts with `mount` and has no `unmount`); the repository's test reads the hooks at a microtask that runs before the write and finds none.
- Unit: eight tests added to `pierre-codeview-bridge.test.js` (the first-drawn stale item, `'unmount'` by id, and a describe "while a list write is drawing": the two restore cases, the item against a kept record that is ahead of it, a command sent during the flush, a list write asked for from the selection callback, a write that throws).
- Mutations: twenty-five, each failing the browser spec and the unit file (`round3/mutations/mutations.py`, `browser-mutations.log`, `unit-mutations.log`). M12 is the code the second verification rejected for finding 1 and M14 the code it rejected for finding 2; M8 is the first round's. The unmutated spec was run before each mutation run. One mutation taught something: the first version of the empty slot relied on the slot's class for its height and failed seven browser tests with the validator's two lines, because nothing gives the slot root a height of its own on a page without the gate's CSS (M22 is that version).

One script, `round3/sequence.sh`, ran everything one after the other on one tree (fingerprint over `tests` and `public` `4ae2128b…` at the start, before and after each E2E run and at the end; `tree-fingerprint.txt`, `e2e-run{1,2}.meta.txt`):

- `pnpm test`: 346 files, 11628 tests, all passing (`unit.log`; 11620 after the first fix round).
- `pnpm run test:contract`: 212 passed (`contract.log`; 200 after the first fix round, +12), validator on in every bridge and gate test; gate measurements re-recorded in `round3/gates/`.
- `pnpm run test:e2e`, two consecutive runs: 852 passed / 2 skipped / 0 failed, twice (854 tests; no E2E test was added or removed).
- Golden dump against phase 3's reference: 32 dumps, 0 unsettled files, 0 console errors, "compared 32 dump(s)", "no regression", exit 0 (`golden/`, `golden-dump.log`, `golden-compare.txt`).

Known flakes at the end of phase 4:

- **Off the list, fixed at the cause:** `hunk-summaries.spec.js:245`, the `mockAppConfig` worker crash, and `team-reviews-filter.spec.js:103` (an interrupted refresh the console guard did not name) (all above).
- The fallback gutter taking a click meant for the hovered row (H54; `comment-crud`, `comments-review`): not seen in this phase's full runs. It goes with the fallback positioner in phase 6.
- `comments-review.spec.js:267`: not a diff spec; once in phase 2; not seen since.
- As before: a failure of any other test in a full run is new information.

Deviations from the plan in this phase, with the proof:

- **`pr.html` and `local.html` were touched** (two script tags each; H101). The plan's two statements about this phase contradict each other; the full E2E runs and the golden dump are the proof that the pages behave as before.
- **`SuggestionManager.createSuggestionRow` was touched** (`suggestion-manager.js` is not in the plan's file list): the orchestrator's instruction for this phase, so that build and sync are one rule. `suggestion-manager.test.js`, `suggestion-sync.test.js` and the E2E suggestion specs pass unchanged.
- **A third upgrade strategy, `'reparse'`, for files without a hunk** (H97), where the plan's table has "none".
- **`whenAnnotationRendered` asks the visibility oracle before it looks at the element** (H94), where the current bridge looks at the element first.
- **`onPostRender` counts as "inside a render pass"** (H99): the plan's depth counter covers three callbacks, the bridge's covers four.
- **A list write asked for from inside a viewer callback is made after the pass, from the records as they are then** (H103). The plan's `_writeList(items)` is a synchronous primitive and the plan does not say what a `renderAll` or `reconcile` from a hook does; written at once it flushes inside the viewer's pass (nine of the eleven tests of `bridge/list-write-in-callback.spec.js` fail with the wait removed, mutation M6). So `_writeList()` takes no list and builds it from `files`; `_requestListWrite` is what the two writers call; the bridge keeps `_listed`, the records the viewer's list was written from, next to `files`; and `scrollToItem`, `scrollToLine`, `selectLines` and `clearLineSelection` wait for a write that is waiting. For a call outside a callback nothing changed: the same records, the same single clear-and-set, the fold before it. Since the second fix round the same holds while a list write is in progress: a list write asked for by the page there (from a hook in its flush, from the selection callback of its view restore) waits for it to end, and a scroll target or selection sent there runs after its view restore.
- **Nothing of the page runs for an item whose record is no longer the pane's, drawn before or not** (H103 rule 3, as corrected by the second fix round; the first report stated it for the header and the host classes only, and it did not hold for an item drawn for the first time in that window). Until the waiting write such an item is drawn from what the bridge holds: its header slot or an empty one of the told height, the host classes it carries, the cards it has. No `renderHeader`, `hostState`, card renderer, card sync, `onLineClick`, `onLineSelect`, `onLineSelectionEnd`, `'mount'` or `'update'` for it.
- **`onItemLifecycle(id, 'unmount')` is told by id**: exactly once for every `'mount'` the page was told, while the pane still has the file; never for a file that was taken out (as before), and never for an item whose `'mount'` was not told. The plan says only that the hook reports mount, update and unmount.
- **The view restore and the scroll and selection commands are decided from the viewer's list** (`_listed` and the viewer's own item), not from `files` (H103 rule 5; second verification, finding 2). Outside the window the two are the same.
- **`destroy()` is final**: `renderAll` answers false and `attach` does nothing afterwards. The plan does not say; a bridge that could be rendered into again after `destroy()` had no listeners and no worker pool, and a waiting list write brought its viewer back (the verifier's P4).
- **A third test-infrastructure flake was fixed** besides the two named for this phase: the console guard names the interrupted team-reviews refresh (`tests/e2e/console-guard.js`, `tests/unit/e2e-console-guard.test.js`; above). It failed one of this round's full runs; the alternative was to report a run with a failure that has nothing to do with the diff.
- **`getAnnotations(file)` does not list the file-comment zone**, and `removeAnnotation` / `removeAnnotationsByType` refuse it: the zone is part of the file, and a consumer that clears "every annotation of a file" must not be able to remove it. A notice is listed.
- **`scrollToLine(file, line, side, options)`** takes `{ scroll, align, behavior, flash }` and still accepts the current bridge's boolean in that position. It answers `isLineVisible`: false for a line hidden in a gap as well as for one that is not in the data (the plan: "false only when the metadata proves the line absent"). The caller's fallback to the file is right in both cases, and a line target on a hidden line would land on the separator (F11).
- **The view restore falls back to the item** when the recorded line is no longer shown after a new diff (a whitespace toggle can remove it): the vendor logs an error for a line target it cannot resolve.
- **`reconcile` takes `{ whenIdle }` and a function**, and the bridge has `holdListWrites()`: the plan's "waits while a pointer button is down and while a `revealInDiff` navigation is in flight" needs something the page can hold; phase 7 holds it around a navigation.
- **Hooks**: `resolvePath` was added (H78 at the bridge's boundary); `hostState` answers a list of class names; `renderHeader` and `hostState` also receive the record's kind.
- **The plan's "rewrite" of four unit files of the current bridge in phase 4** (`pierre-bridge-split`, `-theme`, `-batch-annotations`, `-line-query`) is done as new tests of the new bridge next to them; the current bridge's tests stay until phase 8 deletes the bridge, because it is still the app's engine.
- **F23's failing sequence** is "expand the gap between two ranges from both ends, then Expand all on the gap before the first range", in a file with wrapped lines (H95), not the sequence the amendment's summary describes.
- **Contract-suite files changed besides the new ones**: `harness/constants.js` (re-exports production's `ROWLESS_FIX`; holds `CANDIDATE_CSS`, moved from gate G9 so that G13 uses the same text), `harness/paths.js` (serves `bridge-page.js`), gates G9 and G12 (load the two shared modules the current bridge now needs).

Nothing in this phase changes what a reader sees on the default engine. Nothing user-visible is added, so there is no changeset.

Open items carried forward:

- **Phases 5 to 7**: no hook or card renderer is meant to call `renderAll` or `reconcile`; if one comes to (directly, or through `loadContextFiles` or a refresh), H103's rules are what it gets: the records at once, the list after the pass, and nothing of the page run for an item whose record is no longer the pane's, whether that item was drawn before or is drawn for the first time in that window (no `renderHeader`, `hostState`, card renderer or sync, selection or click callback, `'mount'` or `'update'`; an empty header slot and no cards for one never drawn). The page's `onItemLifecycle` can rely on one `'unmount'` for every `'mount'` it heard of a file the pane still has, and on none for a file it took out. A scroll or a selection sent from a hook while a list write is drawing is applied after that write's view restore. `pr.css` must give `.pr-file-header-slot` its fixed height (gate G9's CSS). A hook must not call `destroy()`.
- **Phase 5**: load `pierre-gutter.js` and `pierre-codeview-bridge.js`; construct the bridge with `root`, `hooks`, `collaborators` and a `keyPrefix` per review; register the `file-comments` and `notice` renderers; the E2E adapter reads `bridge.files` (records: `kind`, `metadata`, `baseMetadata`, `upgraded`, `collapsed`, `annotations`, `storedRanges`, `contextRanges`; `findFileElement(id)` for the host; `isDiffDisplayed(id)` for "settled"); `tests/utils/pierre-bridge-harness.js` and `page-manager.js` still build the current bridge's record and change with the cut-over. `itemMetrics.lineHeight` is measured once (H38). The header token `--pr-file-header-height` must be defined by `pr.css` (the bridge falls back to 45 px without it).
- **Phase 6**: the `sync` of comments and suggestions comes from `collaborators` (`PostedComment`, `suggestionManager`); H71's third item; H45 (button drag over more than two lines); the fallback positioner is not ported.
- **Phase 7**: `holdListWrites()` around `revealInDiff`; H94, H96 (`REVEALED_LINE_KEEPS_FLASH`), H97 against main's golden for hunk-less files; reveal latency against G12.
- **Phase 8**: H93 when the bridge is renamed; H100 and H102; the current bridge's delegations and `_pageEnv` go with it.
- The contract suite is still not run by CI. Q4 is still the user's.

### Phase 5: cut-over 1 behind the switch, the read-only diff

Goal: with `window.__PAIR_REVIEW_DIFF_ENGINE === 'codeview'` (the E2E fixture sets it for `PR_ENGINE=codeview`) the diff pane is drawn by the one virtualized viewer: changed files and context files as items, headers, host stamps, collapse and Viewed, layout and theme, sidebar navigation, notices, the empty and error states, re-render with the view restored, the worker pool. Without the switch nothing a reader sees changes.

What was built:

- **`public/js/pr.js`.** The switch is read once (`_diffEngine`) and branched on in ten methods (correction 14; `tests/unit/engine-switch-sites.test.js`).
  - *The list.* `_buildPaneEntries()` is the one ordered list of the pane (changed files in diff order, each context path before the first entry that sorts after it, the diff wins, no entry for a context path without contents); `_buildDiffEntry(file)` makes a changed file's entry from the render decision main already has (per-file plain-text cap, deferral with its `notice`; no budget across files, decision D3); `fileKind`, `_fileStateKey`, `_effectiveContextRange` (main's end-of-file shift, pure) and `PRManager.countTextLines` (an editor's count, F25) are the resolvers it rests on.
  - *Rendering.* `_renderDiffCodeView(pr)`: a missing file list is not an empty diff (nothing is reset); the tour is left before the list is replaced; headers, zones and collapsed state are dropped; the container is shown before the bridge renders (the viewer measures its rows there); `bridge.renderAll(entries, { keyPrefix: reviewId })`; the context rows are kept and `loadContextFiles({ refreshContents: true })` reads their contents again. Without the viewer's bundle the pane shows `.diff-viewer-missing` in `#diff-status` (decision D1). `renderDiff`'s three shared steps are functions both renderers call (`_abortContentUpgrades`, `_resetHunkSummaryRenderState`, `_startPostRenderLoads`), so the two paths cannot drift.
  - *Headers.* One builder per kind, used by both engines: `_buildFileHeader(file, { collapsed, zone })` and `_buildContextFileHeader(path, { label, zone })`, with the comment and chat buttons they share in `_createFileCommentButton` / `_createFileChatButton` (three copies of that markup on main, one now; H4). `renderFileDiff` and `renderContextFile` call them, markup unchanged. Their controls call functions that work from data, so a header works in a wrapper and in an item's header slot. On `codeview`, `_renderFileHeader(id, kind)` caches one element per file and `_syncFileHeader` re-stamps it from data every time the viewer draws it (chevron, Viewed, file-comment indicator, summary toggle, the badge's title).
  - *Collapsed state as data* (correction 16, H67): `_fileCollapsed`.
  - *Host stamps* (H108): `_fileHostClasses(id)` for the bridge's `hostState` hook, `_setFileElementClass`.
  - *Context files.* `loadContextFiles(options)` branches: the default engine draws wrappers as before (the eviction of a re-ranged file moved in as `_evictRenderedContextFile`, so `ensureContextFile` does not touch the DOM any more); `codeview` runs `_loadContextFilesCodeView` (both generations, contents cached by path with a sequence number that changes with the text, a failed read leaves no item and no cache entry) and then the one `_syncContextItems()` → `bridge.reconcile(...)`. A push (`review:context_files_changed`, the visibility handler) asks with `whenIdle`, so a list replace never lands under a selection being dragged; the comments are then reloaded when it has been applied. `_noteContextItems` compares the context items with those the page last knew and drops header, zone and collapsed state of the ones that went. The dismiss button reads its row ids from `contextFiles` on both engines (`_dismissContextFile`; H86). The context toggles are `setFileCollapsed` and the viewed writer under the context key on `codeview`. `scrollToContextFile` is `_scrollToContextItem`: the header for the file's first range, the first line of a later range, then the flash.
  - *Navigation and layout.* `scrollToFile` addresses the item by its canonical path (`bridge.scrollToItem`, smooth); `handleDiffViewChange` hands the layout to the bridge and returns its answer.
  - *Upgrade trigger.* The bridge's `onItemLifecycle('mount')` queues a changed file for its content upgrade, as a rendered body does on the default engine; the catch-up sweep takes mounted files only (the bridge holds a record of every file). The queue, its limits and its gates are untouched.
  - *Deferred diffs.* The "Large diff ... Load diff" message is the item's `notice` (the element main builds); `_materializeDeferredDiff` goes through the bridge for a record that says `deferred`, with no element (`_materializeDeferredItem`).
  - *Teardown.* `_removeRenderedFiles` gives the viewer an empty list (`_clearDiffItems`); nothing writes into `#diff-container`.
- **`public/js/modules/pierre-codeview-bridge.js`**: correction 12 (`atTop`); a row window of 5,000 rows for every item (`ROWS_PER_ITEM_WINDOW`; F26, H114); `_clearExpansions` resets the item's cached height when it empties a map that had entries (H120).
- **`public/pr.html`, `public/local.html`**: `pierre-gutter.js` and `pierre-codeview-bridge.js`, after the current bridge.
- **`public/css/pr.css`**: one block at the end, every rule scoped by `.diff-view.codeview-engine` (gate G9's CSS, section 6 item 3), plus the token `--pr-file-header-height: 45px` on `:root`. Inside the scroll container `--toolbar-height` and `--tour-bar-offset` are 0 and `--diff-scroll-offset` is the header token, which takes the toolbar and the tour bar out of every sticky `top` and `scroll-margin-top` there without touching those rules.
- **E2E module** (H113): `tests/e2e/diff-dom.js`, `diff-dom-filediff.js`, `diff-dom-codeview.js`.
- **Specs.** N13 `vendor-bundle-missing.spec.js` (new, both modes, both engines). N5 `context-files.spec.js`: three new scenarios, each green on the default engine first (a stored range of a file that cannot be read; a comment stored on the old side of a line; split layout leaves a context file one column as wide as the file), and the edits of correction 13. `diff-render.spec.js`, `diff-theme-highlight.spec.js`, `slim-screen-panels.spec.js`: one edit each (correction 13, H51).
- **`tests/parity/dump.mjs`, `compare.mjs`**: the context stage (two context files, one with two ranges and one whose range runs past the end of its file, and a line comment), landings also measured from whatever is pinned above the diff, and the comparison rules for both.
- **Contract tests** (second session): `tests/contract/vendor/row-window.spec.js` (F26: the separator after a file's last row; the card of a row; the same scrolling with a window larger than the file) and `tests/contract/bridge/view.spec.js` "what is drawn of a mounted file does not change with the scroll position".
- **Unit tests** (new): `engine-switch-sites`, `pane-entries` (with `fileKind`, `_fileStateKey`, `_effectiveContextRange`, `countTextLines`), `load-context-files` (the production bridge over the recording stand-in for the viewer; every sequence of H80), `context-file-header`, `codeview-render-diff`. Adapted: `set-file-collapsed`, `context-entry-collapse`, `SuggestionNavigator`, `diff-status`, `e2e-vendor-dom-boundary`, `pierre-shared-modules`, `pierre-codeview-bridge` (+3), `tests/utils/page-manager.js` and three hand-made factories (`_initPaneState`).

Results of the first session, all from one serial script on one tree (`final-sequence.sh` in the phase-5 evidence; `final-fingerprint.txt` has the fingerprint of everything changed under `public/` and `tests/` at the start, between stages and at the end: `2019ad4acf062500` at all four points):
